Allowed: PDF, JPEG, PNG, GIF, DOC, DOCX.\" };\n706: \n707:   const uploadDir = process.env.UPLOAD_DIR || \"./uploads\";\n708:   await mkdir(uploadDir, { recursive: true });\n709: \n710:   const ext =
